## Using Button Device Design Examples in Fortnite Creative

**כותרת מקורית:** Using Button Device Design Examples in Fortnite Creative  
**כתובת מקור:** https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-button-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative  
**מקור קלט:** `09B_Device_Design_Examples(1).md`  
**היקף:** Creative / Shared בהתאם לעמוד המקור  
**מזהה עמוד:** `using-button-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative`

A **button** is a device you can use to trigger other devices when a player pushes the button.

Here are a couple of examples of how to use Button devices to build gameplay.

-
[Door Hack](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-button-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative)

-
[Team Buttons](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-button-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative)

### Door Hack

Use a button to provide player interaction with a prop by increasing the radius of the prop and adding text for player instruction.

#### Devices Used

-
1 x **Button**

-
1 x [Lock](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-lock-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

#### Build It Yourself

-
Place a Button device over the prop you want to use. In this example, it is a computer on a desk but you can use it with any prop.

-
Customize the button to the following settings:

**אפשרויות, ערכים ותיאורים:**

##### Interact Time

- **Value / Values:** 5.0 Seconds
- **Description:** Time needed to interact with the button before it activates.

##### Trigger Sound

- **Value / Values:** Disabled
- **Description:** No sound effects are played when the button is activated.

##### Interaction Text

- **Value / Values:** Hack Door...
- **Description:** The UI text displayed when the player looks at and interacts with the button. In this case, the text implies the computer is being used to hack.

##### Visible During Game

- **Value / Values:** No
- **Description:** The button is not visible during gameplay.

##### Interaction Radius

- **Value / Values:** 1.0 Meters
- **Description:** The distance from the button the player can be while still bringing up the interaction prompt.

#### -
Place a door in another spot in the room, then place a **Lock** device adjacent to the door, making sure the light turns blue to indicate it is paired with the door.

- **Value / Values:** -
Customize it to the following settings:
- **Description:** Option Value Description
Visible During Game

##### Off

- **Value / Values:** The lock is not visible during gameplay.
- **Description:** -
Set the button's direct event bindings to the following:

#### Function Device Event Description
On Interact Send Event To

- **Value / Values:** LockDevice
- **Description:** Open

##### When the player interacts with the button, the door that the lock is attached to will open.

- **Value / Values:** Here's an overview of how devices communicate in this Design Example:
- **Description:** Device A Function Device B Event Explanation
**LockDevice**

##### Open

- **Value / Values:** **Button**
- **Description:** On Interact Send Event To

##### When the player interacts with the button, the door that the lock is attached to will open.

- **Value / Values:** You now have the basic functionality for players interacting with props using the button.
- **Description:** There are many ways to set up interactive props using the Button device. This device, when invisible, has a highly customizable area to expand interaction coverage, and can be used both narratively and for immersion, or for basic gameplay. [HUD Messages](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-hud-message-devices-in-fortnite-creative) can play in response to player interaction, [Teleporters](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-teleporter-devices-in-fortnite-creative) can be activated, or [**Billboards**](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-billboard-devices-in-fortnite-creative) rendered visible, providing a lot of flexibility for your game design.

### Team Buttons

You can also place buttons that can only be activated by specific teams.

#### Devices Used

-
2 x **Buttons**

-
2 x [Class Selectors](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-class-selector-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

-
2 x [HUD Messages](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-hud-message-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

#### Build It Yourself

-
Place a **Button** device for Team 1. Customize it to the following settings:

**אפשרויות, ערכים ותיאורים:**

##### Interact Time

- **Value / Values:** 3.0 Seconds
- **Description:** Time needed to interact with the button before it activates.

##### Activating Team

- **Value / Values:** Team 1
- **Description:** Only members of Team 1 can interact with this button.

##### Trigger Sound

- **Value / Values:** Disabled
- **Description:** No sound effects play when the button is activated.

#### -
Duplicate the button and place a second one adjacent. Change the **Activating Team** setting on the second button to **Team 2**.

- **Value / Values:** -
Near the button, place a **Class Selector**. Customize it to the following settings:
- **Description:** Option Value Description
Team To Switch To

##### Team 1

- **Value / Values:** When activated, it will switch the player to this team.
- **Description:** Time To Switch

##### Instant

- **Value / Values:** Delay between entering the Class Selector and switching.
- **Description:** -
Copy the Class Selector, and place it near the second button. Change the **Team to Switch To** setting on the second Class Selector to **Team 2**.

#### -
Anywhere on the level, place a **HUD Message**. Customize it to the following settings:

- **Value / Values:** Option Value Description
Message
- **Description:** Team 1 Button activated!

##### The message displayed when the Team 1 Button is activated successfully.

- **Value / Values:** Time From Round Start
- **Description:** Off

##### The message is not automatically played after the start of the round.

- **Value / Values:** Message Priority
- **Description:** Critical

##### The message will overwrite any other HUD Message that is already up.

- **Value / Values:** Placement
- **Description:** Top Center

##### The location on the HUD where the message shows up when the device is activated.

- **Value / Values:** -
Copy the HUD Message device, then place a second device adjacent to the first. Change the **Message** setting on the second HUD Device to **Team 2 Button Activated!**
- **Description:** -
Set the Team 1 Button direct event bindings to the following:

#### Function Device Event Description
On Interact Send Event To

- **Value / Values:** Team1HUDMessage
- **Description:** Show

##### When the player interacts with the button, the corresponding HUD Message will appear.

- **Value / Values:** -
Repeat step 7 with the Team 2 Button and corresponding HUD device.
- **Description:** Here's an overview of how devices communicate in this design example:

#### Device A Function Device B Event Explanation
**Team1 HUD Message**

- **Value / Values:** Show
- **Description:** **Team1 Button**

##### On Interact Send Event To

- **Value / Values:** When the player interacts with the button, the corresponding HUD Message will appear.
- **Description:** **Team2 HUD Message**

##### Show

- **Value / Values:** **Team2 Button**
- **Description:** On Interact Send Event To

##### When the player interacts with the button, the corresponding HUD Message will appear.

- **Value / Values:** You now have the basic structure for using team-restricted buttons to communicate.
- **Description:** Restricting buttons to specific classes or teams is a way to create avenues that are gated behind specific individuals or teams. Doors can be locked except when a button is pressed by a team member, certain classes can cause active defenses to appear, such as hostile sentries, or cause temporary walls and other shifted geometry to change the dynamics of the map in response to gameplay. They can also be used for teleporting to new areas, or equipping new, location-optimized loadouts.

---

## Using Color Changing Tile Device Design Examples in Fortnite Creative

**כותרת מקורית:** Using Color Changing Tile Device Design Examples in Fortnite Creative  
**כתובת מקור:** https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-color-changing-tile-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative  
**מקור קלט:** `09B_Device_Design_Examples(1).md`  
**היקף:** Creative / Shared בהתאם לעמוד המקור  
**מזהה עמוד:** `using-color-changing-tile-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative`

Use the **Color Changing Tile** device to create tiles that change colors when players interact with them.

Here is an example of how you can use the Color Change Tile devices to build some fun gameplay.

### Color Change Minigame

In this example, you'll create a point-scoring mini-game using just three devices, and explore some of the device settings that make the Color Changing Tile device so versatile!

### Devices Used

-
Many [Color Changing Tiles](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-color-changing-tile-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative)

-
2 x [Player Spawners](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-color-changing-tile-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative)

-
2 x [Classic Bouncers ](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-color-changing-tile-device-design-examples-in-fortnite-creative)

For this example, you will construct the play area with tall wall pieces designed to keep players inside the space but in a way that players can also access the tops of the walls.

You will then add color-changing tiles that the players can interact with.

Finally, you will add the player spawners, and bouncers for when a player falls off a wall.

### Build Your Own

Start by making a simple play space for players to move through.

#### Color Changing Tile Devices

-
Once you've built the space, place a **Color Changing Tile **device on the floor between the walls.

-
Customize it with these settings.

 Option Setting Explanation
**Starting Team**

Neutral

This option removes any restrictions on who can score on these tiles.

**Score**

1

Sets a score value.

**Appearance**

Disco

Sets the tile's appearance.

**Display Score Update on HUD**

On

This updates the score on the player's HUD in the course of the game.

**HUD Message**

Delete the text message and leave it blank

The default message is **Score!** but you don't want to display this message each time the player scores on these low-score tiles. You will use the default message later for higher-valued tiles.

-
Copy and place the customized tile to the entire floor area.

-
For variety, copy one tile and rotate it, then add it in random spots to the walls.

-
Add a new Color Changing Tile device on the top of a wall. This device will use different settings, so it's easiest to place a new device entirely. Use the following settings to customize this tile:

 Option Setting Explanation
**Revert Tile**

On

When a player lands on the tile, it changes color. This setting lets it revert to its original color after a specified time.

**Time Until Reverting**

30 seconds

Setting the time to 30 seconds keeps the player from playing the same tile over and over for points.

**Score**

10

This tile is worth 10 points (compared to 1 point for the earlier tiles).

**Steal Score**

No

Once a player has scored on this tile, another player cannot steal it, but has to wait until the 30 seconds are up and the tile reverts.

**Display Score Update on HUD**

On

This sends the score earned to the HUD.

**HUD Message**

Score!

This is the default message, and for the higher-scoring tiles, you want this to display when the player scores.

**Alternate Tile Shape**

Flat Hexagon

This changes the shape of the tile. Since it has a different value from the floor and wall tiles, changing the shape will call attention to it.

**Alternate Tile Shape Icon**

Star

Again, adding an icon to the top of the tile helps to catch the player's attention.

**Alternate Tile Shape Starting Top Color**

#000000 (black)

If the primary color for the icon is white (the default color) then you need to add a contrasting color to make the icon visible.

Once you've customized the tile, copy and place these higher-scoring tiles at interesting places on tops of the arena walls.

#### Player Spawner and Bouncer Devices

-
Add two **Player Spawner** devices — one on either side of the play space, on a wall top. These spawners don't need to be customized.

-
Add classic bouncer pads from the **Bouncer Gallery** beneath each spawner. This is for players to use if they fall off the wall and need a way to get back into the play area. The bouncer also doesn't need any customization.

You're almost done!

#### Island Settings Modifications

-
Go to **Island Settings** and select the **Mode** category. Under **Structure**, make sure the **Total Rounds** is set to **1**.

-
Under **Victory Condition**, change the **Game Win Condition** to **Most Score Wins**.

-
Go to the **Round** category. Under **End Condition**, set a **Time Limit** of **2 minutes**, then verify that the **Timer Direction** is set to **Count Down**.

And that's all there is to it — you've just created your own minigame based on the Color Changing Tile device!

### Design Tips

-
For more variety, try adding weapons to your play space.

-
You could also copy/paste your small arena to expand the play area!

[
*](https://dev.epicgames.com/community/api/documentation/image/b7d42392-6a35-4847-9c24-f79360d7181d?resizing_type=fit)

---

## VFX Spawner Device Design Examples in Fortnite

**כותרת מקורית:** VFX Spawner Device Design Examples in Fortnite  
**כתובת מקור:** https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/vfx-spawner-device-design-examples-in-fortnite  
**מקור קלט:** `09B_Device_Design_Examples(1).md`  
**היקף:** Creative / Shared בהתאם לעמוד המקור  
**מזהה עמוד:** `vfx-spawner-device-design-examples-in-fortnite`

**
You can use the VFX Spawner **device to give players feedback, reinforce the theme of a space, or signal gameplay events by using special effects.

The examples here show three common use cases: a finish-line effect, a destruction game with prop-based reactions, and a healing zone with layered ambient and interactive effects.

### Finish Line Effects

Use the VFX Spawner to quickly set up effects that give players feedback. In this example, you'll create a finish line trigger for a race that starts fireworks.

#### Devices Used

-
1 x [VFX Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-vfx-spawner-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

-
1 x [Player Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-player-spawn-pad-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

-
1 x [Trigger ](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-trigger-devices-in-fortnite-creative)device

#### Set Up the Devices

-
Place a **Player Spawner** device.

-
Place a finish line asset from the **Obstacle Course Parts Gallery**.

-
Place a **Trigger **and size it to cover the entire finish line.
**

-
Customize the Trigger as follows:

 Option Value
Visible In Game

Off

Trigger VFX

Off

Trigger SFX

Off

-
Place a VFX Spawner** in the sky above the finish line.

-
Customize the VFX Spawner as follows:

 Option Value
Visual Effect

FireworksLarge

Enabled on Phase

None

-
Configure the following event on the Trigger so that the VFX Spawner starts when the player crosses the finish line.

 Event Select Device Select Function
On Triggered Send Event To

VFX Spawner

Enable

You now have a working finish line effect.

#### Design Tip

Fireworks are an effective way to reward a player for completing a goal, but there are many other visual effects to explore. Try using the VFX Spawner to create ambient particle effects that change the feel of a space — like the fog effect, for example.

### Build a Transformer Destruction Game

VFX Spawners can also be configured to use a one-time burst effect, such as an explosion. In this example, you’ll use that functionality (along with some other VFX Spawners for additional effects) to create a game mode where the player must destroy transformers!

#### Devices Used

-
10 x VFX Spawners

-
1 x Player Spawner

-
1 x [Item Granter](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-item-granter-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

-
1 x [Tracker](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-tracker-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

-
1 x [Timer](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-timer-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

-
5 x [Beacons](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-beacon-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

-
5 x [Prop Manipulators](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-prop-manipulator-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

#### Set Up the Basic Gameplay

-
Start with the **Tilted Towers POI Island** starter island.

-

#### Configure the Transformers

-
Place a **transformer prop** from the **Power Plant Prop Gallery**.

-

-
Place a VFX Spawner** on top of the transformer. This creates the sparking effect when the transformer is damaged.

-

-
Place another **VFX Spawner** in the middle of the transformer. This creates the lightning explosion when the transformer is destroyed.

-

-

Configure the following events on the Prop Manipulator. When the transformer is damaged, the sparking effect plays. When it's destroyed, the lightning explosion plays, the Tracker increments the player's progress, and the Beacon and sparking effect are disabled.

-
Select all of these devices — the Prop Manipulator, two VFX Spawners, Beacon, and transformer prop — and copy and paste them four more times around the map.

You now have a minigame where the player destroys transformers within a time limit.

#### Design Tip

The VFX Spawner pairs well with the Prop Manipulator for showing, hiding, and destroying objects. A well-timed burst effect makes these moments feel more natural and impactful, so keep this combo in mind!

### Build a Survival Game with a Healing Zone

The VFX Spawner works with other devices to indicate different functionality for different areas of a map. In this example, you'll use VFX Spawners and other devices to create a healing zone in a survival game.

#### Devices Used

-

-
1 x Volume

#### Configure the Healing Zone

There are three VFX Spawners for different purposes in the healing zone:

-
**Ambient VFX Spawner:** Plays a continuous effect to indicate to the player where the zone is when they're outside it.

-
**Enter Healing Zone VFX Spawner: **Plays a burst effect to indicate to the player that they've entered the zone and healing has started.

-
**Healing VFX Spawner: **Plays a continuous effect to indicate to the player that they're inside the zone and healing.

-
In the middle of the basketball court, place a **VFX Spawner**. This will be the **Ambient VFX Spawner**.

-
Customize the VFX Spawner as follows:
**

 Option Value
Visual Effect

Embers

Colorize VFX

On

Custom Color

Apple Green

-
Place another VFX Spawner. This will be the Enter Healing Zone VFX Spawner**.

-
Customize the VFX Spawner as follows:
**

Effect Type

Burst

Burst Visual Effect

Explosion Electrical

On

Custom Color

Apple Green

-
Place one more VFX Spawner. This will be the Healing VFX Spawner**.

-
Customize the VFX Spawner as follows:

 Option Value

Apple Green

-
Configure the following event on the Volume so that when the player enters it, the Health Powerup triggers, the Enter Healing Zone VFX plays, the Healing VFX begins playing, and the Ambient VFX stops playing. When the player leaves the zone, the Health Powerup stops, the Healing VFX stops, and the Ambient VFX starts playing again.

Healing VFX Spawner

Disable

On Exit Send Event To

You now have a survival game with a well-indicated healing zone!

#### Design Tip

VFX is a great, subtle way to hint to a player that they should check out a certain area of the map. Outside of a healing zone, try using the VFX Spawner to guide players toward new areas that they should explore or difficult to find collectible items!

## Visual Effect Powerup Device Design Examples in Fortnite

**כותרת מקורית:** Visual Effect Powerup Device Design Examples in Fortnite  
**כתובת מקור:** https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/visual-effect-powerup-device-design-examples-in-fortnite  
**מקור קלט:** `09B_Device_Design_Examples(1).md`  
**היקף:** Creative / Shared בהתאם לעמוד המקור  
**מזהה עמוד:** `visual-effect-powerup-device-design-examples-in-fortnite`

###### Prerequisite topics

In order to understand and use the content on this page, make sure you are familiar with the following topics:

- [Visual Effect Powerup Devices](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-visual-effect-powerup-devices-in-fortnite-creative)

**
You can set a Visual Effect Powerup** device to validate player behavior with a visual effect, such as when the player picks up an item or reaches a destination. Use this device to reinforce in-game information, communicate the importance of an event, or make characters sparkle just for fun!

### Basic Setup

Think of the [Visual Effect Powerup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-visual-effect-powerup-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device as the location where you set up the details of the effect that you want to play on characters in-game. Like other devices that players never need to see or interact with, it should be placed somewhere convenient for the island creator to access and change, but it does not need to be in the playable area of your island.

#### Devices Used

-
1 x [Visual Effect Powerup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-visual-effect-powerup-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

-
1 x [Health Powerup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-health-powerup-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

#### Overview

-
Place the Visual Effect Powerup device.

-
Place the Health Powerup device.

-
Customize the settings.

-
Configure the functions.

#### Place and Customize Devices

-
Place the **Visual Effects Powerup** device in a convenient location.

-
Place a **Health Powerup** device near it.

-
Customize the Visual Effects Powerup device with the following settings:

 Option Value
Effect Duration

10 Seconds

Respawn

YES

Time to Respawn

15 Seconds

Visual Effect

GLOW

Color Type

DIRECT COLOR

Custom Color

#00F471

#### Set the Functions for the Devices

-
Link the **Visual Effect Powerup** to the **Health Powerup** using the following functions settings:

 Function Select Device Select Event
Pickup When Receiving From

Health Powerup

On Item Picked Up

-
Configure the function settings for the Health powerup as well:

 Function Select Device Select Event
Pickup When Receiving From

Visual Effect Powerup Device

On Item Picked Up

#### Design Tip

Choose the colors and visual effects you like for the powerups!

### Combine Powerups for Visual Effects

Using techniques from the [basic setup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/visual-effect-powerup-device-design-examples-in-fortnite#basic-setup) above, you can add visual effects to other powerup devices.

This example is a good follow to the previous example.

Devices Used

-

-

-
1 x [Grind Powerup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/grind-powerup-devices) device

-
3 x Visual Effects Powerup devices

#### Overview

-

-
Place a Visual Effect Powerup devices near each one.

-
Configure the Visual Effect Powerup devices.

-
Configure the functions for each Visual Effects Powerup device.

#### Place the Devices

-
Place a **Damage Amplifier Powerup** device, a **Stat Powerup** device, and a **Grind Powerup** device in a convenient location.

-
Place a **Visual Effect Powerup** device near each one.
**

 Place these devices near the powerups in step 1 for convenience.

**

Configure the functions:

 Function Select Device Select Event

**

Now you have set up distinctive visual effects for every powerup on your island!

Try experimenting with the duration of the powerups and the visual effects.

### Create a More Complex Setup

Let’s create a wild free-for-all battle game mode for up to 8 players!

#### Devices Used

-
10 x Item Spawner devices

-
8 x Player Spawner devices

-
2 x [Cannon Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-cannon-spawner-devices-in-fortnite-creative) devices

-
2 x [Siege Cannon Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-siege-cannon-devices-in-fortnite-creative) devices

-
1 x [Boat Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-boat-spawner-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

-
1 x [Helicopter Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-helicopter-spawner-devices-in-fortnite-creative) device

-
2 x [Surfboard Spawner](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-surfboard-spawner-devices-in-fortnite-creative) devices

-
12 x Visual Effect Powerup devices

-
1 x [Barrier ](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/using-barrier-devices-in-fortnite-creative)device

#### Overview

-
Make a ship-themed battle arena.

-
Customize the Visual Effect Powerup devices and powerups.

-
Place the Player Spawners.

-
Place the Vehicle Spawners.

-
Place the Item Spawners.

-
Place the powerups.

-
Configure the game mode.

#### Make a Ship-Themed Battle Arena
**

Oasis Island features a tiny island with a handy beached boat. That island makes a good mid-point for our ship-to-ship island battle!

-
Start with the Oasis Island** starter island.

-
Use pieces from the **Megalodon **and **Lazy Lagoon** galleries to create the play area. Place the Megalodon super-ship and the pirate ship prefabs on either side of Oasis Island.
**

-
In this example, stairs from these galleries were used to create access ramps from the island up onto each ship.

-
To get a look like the example, go to Island Settings > World > Ambience**, then use the following values:

 Option Value
Time of Day

5:00 PM

Light Color

YELLOW

Fog Color

GREEN

#### Customize the Visual Effect Powerup Devices for All Four Powerups

This step is the same as [Basic Setup](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/visual-effect-powerup-device-design-examples-in-fortnite#basic-setup) and [Combine Powerups for Visual Effects](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/fortnite/visual-effect-powerup-device-design-examples-in-fortnite#combine-powerups-for-visual-effects), so repeat those steps here.

#### Place the Player Spawners

Place five player spawners on the Megalodon ship and three more spawners on the pirate ship.

Pick whatever starting locations seem fun to you!

#### Place the Vehicle Spawners

This play space is so big that it’s perfect for using vehicles to add to the fun!

-
Place the **Helicopter Spawner** and the **Siege Cannon Spawner** on the bow of the ship.
**

-
Add a second Siege Cannon Spawner** on the back deck.
**

-
Add a Boat Spawner** by the mouth of the ship.
**

-
Add two Cannon Spawners** below decks.

#### Load and Place the Item Spawners

-
Make weapons and equipment available to your players by dropping it onto an **Item Spawner** device. Use whatever weapons and equipment you prefer.

-
Configure the Item Spawner:

 Option Value
Random Spawns

RANDOM

Item Scale

1.5

-
Place copies of your Item Spawner device all over your island in interesting and fun locations. This example has 10 spawners, with some on both ships and one in the middle of Oasis Island itself.

#### Place the Powerups in the Play Area

Copy and place the various powerup spawners where you think players would have the most fun finding and using:

#### Configure the Game Mode Using the Island Settings

-
Go to Island Settings > Mode > Structure > Max Players and select **8**:
**

-
 Go to Mode > Matchmaking Settings** and select:
**

 Option Value
Island Matchmaking Privacy

Party Choice

Minimum Players

2

Overtiime Player Target

2

-
Go to Scoring > Elimination Scoring** and select **2**:
**

-
Go to Mode > Victory Condition > Game Win Condition** and select **Most Score Wins:**

#### Add a Barrier Device to Constrain the Action

To help ensure that players don’t wander too far away from the fun, add a barrier device just under Oasis Island in the center of the map:

Use the following settings on the device:

 Option Value
Barrier Material

Translucent (only visible when near)

Zone Shape

Hollow Box

Barrier Depth

60.0 Tiles

Barrier Width

60.0 Tiles

Barrier Height

30 Tiles

Collide With Camera

Off

#### Design Tip

And there you have it — a unique, playable, free-for-all fight for up to eight players!

Try adjusting the placement of the powerups, vehicles, even the weapons — inside of the Item Spawners to create unique experiences.
